\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{3})\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\,\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|^{2}\lambda_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}Y_{4}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|^{2}\lambda_{1},\quad

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{4})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{6}\,\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|^{3}\lambda_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}Y_{5}|

\displaystyle=0,\quad

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{5})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{6}\,\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|^{3}\lambda_{1}

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}Y_{6}|

\displaystyle=0,\quad

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{6})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{12}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|^{6}.

As a result,

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}U_{a}|\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|^{2}\lambda_{1}=o(\tilde{\lambda}^{4}).

(E.25)

Also using Corollary
E.1
and that
|\tilde{\lambda}|/\sqrt{\lambda_{1}}\to\infty
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(U_{a})\lesssim\lambda_{1}^{4}+|\tilde{\lambda}|^{3}\lambda_{1}^{2}+|\tilde{\lambda}|^{6}.

(E.26)

The terms below appear in Lemma G.7 of
Jin et al. 2021c
. The bounds on
Z_{1}
and
Z_{2}
are quoted directly from
Jin et al. 2021c
.

Lemma E.9
.

Under the alternative hypothesis, it holds that

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}Z_{1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\lesssim\lambda_{1}^{2},\qquad

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{1})\lesssim\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}\,\|\theta\|_{3}^{6}\lesssim\lambda_{1}^{4}

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}Z_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\lesssim\lambda_{1}^{2},\quad

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{2})\lesssim\frac{\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}\,\|\theta\|_{3}^{3}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}\lesssim\lambda_{1}^{3}

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}Z_{3}|

\displaystyle=0,\quad

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{3})\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\,\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|^{2}\lambda_{1}^{3}

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}Z_{4}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\,\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|\lambda_{1},\quad

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{4})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\,\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|^{2}\lambda_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}Z_{5}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\,\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|^{2}\lambda_{1},\quad

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{5})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{8}\,\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|^{4}\lambda_{1}

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}Z_{6}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\,\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|^{2},\quad

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{6})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{8}\,\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|^{4}.

Using Corollary
E.1
and the fact that
|\tilde{\lambda}|/\sqrt{\lambda_{1}}\to\infty
under the alternative hypothesis, we have

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}U_{b}|

\displaystyle\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|^{2}\lambda_{1},

(E.27)

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(U_{b})

\displaystyle\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|^{2}\lambda_{1}^{3}.

(E.28)

The terms below appear in Lemma G.9 of
Jin et al. 2021c
. The bounds on
T_{1}
and
F
are quoted directly from
Jin et al. 2021c
since they do not depend on
\tilde{O}mega
.

Lemma E.10
.

Under the alternative hypothesis, it holds that

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}T_{1}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}\lesssim\lambda_{1},\quad

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(T_{1})\lesssim\frac{\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}\,\|\theta\|_{3}^{3}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}\lesssim\lambda_{1}^{3}

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}T_{2}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\,\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|,\quad

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(T_{2})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\,\|\theta\|_{2}^{8}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|^{2}\lambda_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}F|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\lesssim\lambda_{1}^{2},\quad

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(F)\lesssim\frac{\|\theta\|_{2}^{10}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}\lesssim\lambda_{1}^{3}

Using Corollary
E.1
and the fact that
|\tilde{\lambda}|/\sqrt{\lambda_{1}}\to\infty
under the alternative hypothesis, we have

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}U_{c}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{1}^{2},

(E.29)

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(U_{c})

\displaystyle\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|^{2}\lambda_{1}^{2}.

(E.30)

Using Corollary
E.1
and that
|\tilde{\lambda}|/\sqrt{\lambda_{1}}\to\infty
, the inequalities (
E.25
)–(
E.30
) imply Theorem
E.3
.

E.3.3
Real SgnQ

Our first lemma regarding real SgnQ plays the part of Lemma G.11 from
Jin et al. 2021c
.

Lemma E.11
.

Under the previous assumptions, as
n\to\infty
,

•

Under the null hypothesis,
|\mathbb{E}[Q^{*}-\tilde{Q}^{*}]|=o(\|\theta\|_{2}^{4})

and
\mathrm{Var}(Q^{*}-\tilde{Q}^{*})=o(\|\theta\|_{2}^{8})
.

•

Under the alternative hypothesis, if
|\tilde{\lambda}|/\sqrt{\lambda_{1}}\to\infty
, then
|\mathbb{E}[Q^{*}-\tilde{Q}^{*}]|\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|^{2}\lambda_{1}

and
\mathrm{Var}(Q^{*}-\tilde{Q}^{*})\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|^{2}\lambda_{1}^{3}
.

The following lemma plays the part of Lemma G.12 from
Jin et al. 2021c
.

Lemma E.12
.

Under the previous assumptions, as
n\to\infty
,

•

Under the null hypothesis,
|\mathbb{E}[Q-\tilde{Q}^{*}]|=o(\|\theta\|_{2}^{4})

and
\mathrm{Var}(Q-\tilde{Q}^{*})=o(\|\theta\|_{2}^{8})
.

•

Under the alternative hypothesis, if
|\tilde{\lambda}|/\sqrt{\lambda_{1}}\to\infty
, then
|\mathbb{E}[Q-\tilde{Q}^{*}]|\lesssim\lambda_{1}^{2}+|\tilde{\lambda}|^{3}

and
\mathrm{Var}(Q-\tilde{Q}^{*})\lesssim\lambda_{1}^{4}
.

E.4
Proofs of Lemmas
E.7
–
E.12

E.4.1
Proof strategy

First we describe our method of proof for Lemmas
E.7
–
E.10
. We borrow the following strategy from
Jin et al. 2021c
. Let
T
denote a term appearing in one of the Lemmas
E.7
–
E.10
, which takes the general form

\displaystyle T=\sum_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}\in\mathcal{R}}c_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}}G_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}}

where

•

m=O(1)
,

•

\mathcal{R}
is a subset of
[n]^{m}
,

•

c_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}}=\prod_{(s,s^{\prime})\in A}\Gamma_{i_{s},i_{s^{\prime}}}^{(s,s^{\prime})}
is a nonstochastic coefficient where
A\subset[m]\times[m]
and

\Gamma^{(s,s^{\prime})}\in\{\tilde{\Omega},\eta^{*}\mathbf{1}^{\mathsf{T}},\eta\mathbf{1}^{\mathsf{T}},\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\mathsf{T}}\}
, and

•

G_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}}=\prod_{(s,s^{\prime})\in B}W_{i_{s},i_{s^{\prime}}}
where
B\subset[m]\times[m]
.

Since we are studying signed quadrilateral, one can simply take
m=4
above, though we wish to state the lemma in a general way.

Define a
canonical upper bound

\overline{\Gamma_{i_{s},i_{s^{\prime}}}^{(s,s^{\prime})}}
(up to constant factor) on
\Gamma_{i_{s},i_{s^{\prime}}}^{(s,s^{\prime})}
as follows:

\displaystyle\overline{\Gamma_{i_{s},i_{s^{\prime}}}^{(s,s^{\prime})}}=\begin{cases}\beta_{i_{s}}\theta_{i_{s}}\beta_{i_{s^{\prime}}}\theta_{i_{s^{\prime}}}\quad&\text{if }\Gamma^{(s,s^{\prime})}=\tilde{\Omega},\\
\theta_{i_{s}}\quad&\text{if }\Gamma^{(s,s^{\prime})}\in\{\eta^{*}\mathbf{1}^{\mathsf{T}},\eta\mathbf{1}^{\mathsf{T}}\}\\
1\quad&\text{otherwise}.\end{cases}

(E.31)

Define

\displaystyle\overline{c_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}}}=\prod_{(s,s^{\prime})\in A}\overline{\Gamma_{i_{s},i_{s^{\prime}}}^{(s,s^{\prime})}}.

(E.32)

By Corollary
E.1
and Lemma
E.6
,

\displaystyle|c_{i_{1},\ldots i_{m}}|\lesssim\overline{c_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}}}.

In
Jin et al. 2021c
, each term
T
is decomposed into a sum of
L=O(1)
terms:

\displaystyle T=\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}T^{(L)}=\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\,\sum_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}\in\mathcal{R}^{(\ell)}}c_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}}G_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}}.

(E.33)

In our analysis below and that of
Jin et al. 2021c
, an upper bound
\overline{\mathbb{E}T}
on
|\mathbb{E}T|
is obtained by

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}T|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}|\mathbb{E}T^{(\ell)}|\leq\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\,\,\sum_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}\in\mathcal{R}^{(\ell)}}|c_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}}|\cdot|\mathbb{E}G_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}}|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\,\,\sum_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}\in\mathcal{R}^{(\ell)}}\overline{c_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}}}\cdot|\mathbb{E}G_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}}|

\displaystyle=:\overline{\mathbb{E}T}.

(E.34)

Also an upper bound
\overline{\mathrm{Var}T}
on
\mathrm{Var}T
is obtained by

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}T

\displaystyle\leq L\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\mathrm{Var}(T^{(\ell)})

\displaystyle\leq L\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\,\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}\in\mathcal{R}^{(\ell)}\\
i_{1}^{\prime},\ldots,i_{m}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{R}^{(\ell)}\end{subarray}}|c_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}}c_{i_{1}^{\prime},\ldots,i_{m}^{\prime}}|\cdot\big|\mathrm{Cov}\big(G_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}},G_{i_{1}^{\prime},\ldots,i_{m}^{\prime}}\big)\big|

\displaystyle\leq L\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\,\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}\in\mathcal{R}^{(\ell)}\\
i_{1}^{\prime},\ldots,i_{m}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{R}^{(\ell)}\end{subarray}}\overline{c_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}}}\cdot\overline{c_{i_{1}^{\prime},\ldots,i_{m}^{\prime}}}\cdot\big|\mathrm{Cov}\big(G_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}},G_{i_{1}^{\prime},\ldots,i_{m}^{\prime}}\big)\big|

\displaystyle=:\overline{\mathrm{Var}T}.

(E.35)

In Lemmas
E.7
–
E.10
, all stated upper bounds are obtained in this manner and are therefore upper bounds on
\overline{\mathbb{E}T}
and
\overline{\mathrm{Var}T}
.

Note that the definition of
\overline{\mathbb{E}T}
and
\overline{\mathrm{Var}T}
depends on the specific decomposition (
E.33
) of
T
given in
Jin et al. 2021c
. Refer to the proofs below for details including the explicit decomposition. Again we remark that the difference between our setting and
Jin et al. 2021c
is that the canonical upper bound on
|\tilde{\Omega}_{ij}|
used in
Jin et al. 2021c
is of the form
\alpha\theta_{i}\theta_{j}
rather than the inequality
\beta_{i}\theta_{i}\beta_{j}\theta_{j}
which is required for our purposes.

The formalism above immediately yields the following useful fact that allows us to transfer bounds between terms that have similar structures.

Lemma E.13
.

Suppose that

\displaystyle T

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}\in\mathcal{R}}c_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}}G_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}},

\displaystyle T^{*}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}\in\mathcal{R}}c^{*}_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}}G_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}},

where

\displaystyle|c_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\overline{c^{*}_{i_{1},\ldots,i_{m}}}

Then

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}T|\lesssim\overline{\mathbb{E}[T^{*}]}

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\,T\lesssim\overline{\mathrm{Var}\,T^{*}}.

In the second part of our analysis, we show that Lemmas
E.11
and
E.12
follow from Lemmas
E.7
–
E.10
and repeated applications of Lemma
E.13
.

E.4.2
Proof of Lemma
E.7

The bounds for
X_{1}
follow immediately from
Jin et al. 2021c
.

In
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.37)
it is shown that

\mathbb{E}X_{2}=0
, and

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(X_{2})=2\sum_{i,j,k,\ell(dist.)}\tilde{\Omega}_{ij}^{2}\cdot\mathrm{Var}(W_{jk}W_{k\ell}W_{\ell i}).

Thus by (
E.1
) and (
E.2
),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(X_{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i,j,k,\ell(dist.)}\tilde{\Omega}_{ij}^{2}\cdot\mathrm{Var}(W_{jk}W_{k\ell}W_{\ell i})\lesssim\sum_{i,j,k,\ell}\beta_{i}^{2}\theta_{i}^{2}\beta_{j}^{2}\theta_{j}^{2}\cdot\Omega_{jk}\Omega_{k\ell}\Omega_{\ell i}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i,j,k,\ell}\beta_{i}^{2}\theta_{i}^{2}\beta_{j}^{2}\theta_{j}^{2}\cdot\theta_{j}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{i}=\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\,\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}

In
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg. 38)
it is shown that
\mathbb{E}X_{3}=0
and

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(X_{3})

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i,k,\ell(dist)}\big(\sum_{j\notin\{i,k,\ell\}}\tilde{\Omega}_{ij}\tilde{\Omega}_{jk}\big)^{2}\cdot\mathrm{Var}(W_{k\ell}W_{\ell i}).

By (
E.20
) and (
E.24
),

\displaystyle\big(\sum_{j\notin\{i,k,\ell\}}\tilde{\Omega}_{ij}\tilde{\Omega}_{jk}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\beta_{i}^{2}\theta_{i}^{2}\,\beta_{k}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\,\,\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}

Thus by (
E.1
) and (
E.2
),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(X_{3})

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i,k,\ell}\beta_{i}^{2}\theta_{i}^{2}\,\beta_{k}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\,\,\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\cdot\Omega_{k\ell}\Omega_{\ell i}\lesssim\sum_{i,k,\ell}\beta_{i}^{2}\theta_{i}^{3}\,\beta_{k}^{2}\theta_{k}^{3}\,\,\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\cdot\theta_{\ell}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{8}\,\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}.

In
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg. 38)
it is shown that
\mathbb{E}X_{4}=0
and

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(X_{4})\lesssim\sum_{i,j,k,\ell(dist.)}\tilde{\Omega}_{ij}^{2}\tilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}^{2}\cdot\mathrm{Var}(W_{jk}W_{\ell i}).

By (
E.1
) and (
E.20
),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(X_{4})

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i,j,k,\ell}\beta_{i}^{2}\theta_{i}^{2}\beta_{j}^{2}\theta_{j}^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\beta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\cdot\theta_{j}\theta_{k}\theta_{\ell}\theta_{i}\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{8}.

In
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg. 39)
it is shown that
\mathbb{E}X_{5}=0
and

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(X_{5})

\displaystyle=2\sum_{i<\ell}\big(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,k\notin\{i,\ell\}\\
j\neq k\end{subarray}}\tilde{\Omega}_{ij}\tilde{\Omega}_{jk}\tilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}\big)^{2}\cdot\mathrm{Var}(W_{\ell i}).

We have

\displaystyle\big|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,k\notin\{i,\ell\}\\
j\neq k\end{subarray}}\tilde{\Omega}_{ij}\tilde{\Omega}_{jk}\tilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}\big|\lesssim\beta_{i}\theta_{i}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\beta_{\ell}\theta_{\ell}.

Thus by (
E.1
) and (
E.2
),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(X_{5})\lesssim\sum_{i,\ell}\big(\beta_{i}\theta_{i}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\beta_{\ell}\theta_{\ell}\big)^{2}\cdot\theta_{\ell}\theta_{i}\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{12}.

Note that
X_{6}
is a nonstochastic term. Mimicking
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg. 39)
, we have by (
E.24
),

\displaystyle|X_{6}-\tilde{\lambda}^{4}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i,j,k,\ell(not\,dist.)}\beta_{i}^{2}\theta_{i}^{2}\beta_{j}^{2}\theta_{j}^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\beta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\lesssim\sum_{i,j,k}\beta_{i}^{2}\theta_{i}^{2}\beta_{j}^{2}\theta_{j}^{2}\beta_{k}^{4}\theta_{k}^{4}\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{6}\lesssim|\tilde{\lambda}|^{3}.

This completes the proof. ∎

E.4.3
Proof of Lemma
E.8

The bounds on
Y_{1}
carry over directly from
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Lemma G.5)
.

In
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg. 43)
it is shown that
\mathbb{E}Y_{2}=0
. To study
\mathrm{Var}(Y_{2})
, we write
Y=Y_{2a}+Y_{2b}+Y_{2c}
where as in
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg. 43)
, we define

\displaystyle Y_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\sqrt{v}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,k,\ell(dist)\\
s\neq j\end{subarray}}\eta_{i}\widetilde{\Omega}_{jk}W_{js}W_{k\ell}W_{\ell i}

(E.38)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\sqrt{v}}\sum_{i,k,\ell(dist)}\Bigl(\sum_{j\notin\{i,k,\ell\}}\eta_{j}\widetilde{\Omega}_{jk}\Bigr)W^{2}_{i\ell}W_{k\ell}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\sqrt{v}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,k,\ell(dist)\\
s\notin\{i,\ell\}\end{subarray}}\Bigl(\sum_{j\notin\{i,k,\ell\}}\eta_{j}\widetilde{\Omega}_{jk}\Bigr)W_{is}W_{k\ell}W_{\ell i}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle Y_{2a}+Y_{2b}+Y_{2c}.

(E.39)

There it is shown that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{2a})\lesssim\frac{1}{v}\sum_{ijk\ell s}\big|\eta_{i}\tilde{\Omega}_{jk}+\eta_{i}\tilde{\Omega}_{sk}+\eta_{k}\tilde{\Omega}_{ji}+\eta_{k}\tilde{\Omega}_{si}\big|^{2}\cdot\mathrm{Var}(W_{js}W_{k\ell}W_{\ell i}).

We have by (
E.22
)

\displaystyle\big|\eta_{i}\tilde{\Omega}_{jk}+\eta_{i}\tilde{\Omega}_{sk}+\eta_{k}\tilde{\Omega}_{ji}+\eta_{k}\tilde{\Omega}_{si}\big|\lesssim\theta_{i}\beta_{j}\theta_{j}\beta_{k}\theta_{k}+\theta_{i}\beta_{s}\theta_{s}\beta_{k}\theta_{k}+\theta_{k}\beta_{j}\theta_{j}\beta_{i}\theta_{i}+\theta_{k}\beta_{s}\theta_{s}\beta_{i}\theta_{i}.

Hence by (
E.1
), (
E.2
), and (
E.18
),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{2a})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{v}\sum_{ijk\ell s}\big(\theta_{i}\beta_{j}\theta_{j}\beta_{k}\theta_{k}+\theta_{i}\beta_{s}\theta_{s}\beta_{k}\theta_{k}+\theta_{k}\beta_{j}\theta_{j}\beta_{i}\theta_{i}+\theta_{k}\beta_{s}\theta_{s}\beta_{i}\theta_{i}\big)^{2}\cdot\theta_{j}\theta_{s}\theta_{k}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{i}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}

Next, in
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg. 43)
, it is shown that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{2b})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}ik\ell(dist)\\
i^{\prime}k^{\prime}\ell^{\prime}(dist)\end{subarray}}|\alpha_{ik\ell}\alpha_{i^{\prime}k^{\prime}\ell^{\prime}}|\cdot\mathbb{E}[W_{i\ell}^{2}W_{k\ell},W_{i^{\prime}\ell^{\prime}}^{2}W_{k^{\prime}\ell^{\prime}}]

where
\alpha_{ik\ell}=\sum_{j\notin\{i,k,\ell\}}\eta_{j}\tilde{\Omega}_{jk}
. By (
E.24
),

\displaystyle|\alpha_{ik\ell}|\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}\|\theta\|_{2}\,\theta_{k}.

By (
E.1
), (
E.18
), the inequalities above, and the casework in
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.44)
on
E[W_{i\ell}^{2}W_{k\ell},W_{i^{\prime}\ell^{\prime}}^{2}W_{k^{\prime}\ell^{\prime}}]
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{2b})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}ik\ell(dist)\\
i^{\prime}k^{\prime}\ell^{\prime}(dist)\end{subarray}}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}\theta_{k}\theta_{k^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}[W_{i\ell}^{2}W_{k\ell},W_{i^{\prime}\ell^{\prime}}^{2}W_{k^{\prime}\ell^{\prime}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}}{v}\big(\sum_{ik\ell}\theta_{i}\theta_{k}^{3}\theta_{\ell}^{2}+\sum_{ik\ell i^{\prime}}\theta_{i}\theta_{k}^{3}\theta_{\ell}^{3}\theta_{i^{\prime}}+\sum_{ik\ell}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}.

Next, in
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.44)
it is shown that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{2c})\lesssim\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}ik\ell(dist)\\
s\notin\{i,\ell\}\end{subarray}}\beta^{2}_{ik\ell}\mathrm{Var}(W_{is}W_{k\ell}W_{\ell i})

where
\alpha_{ik\ell}
is defined the same as with
Y_{2b}
. Thus

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{2c})\lesssim\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}ik\ell(dist)\\
s\notin\{i,\ell\}\end{subarray}}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\cdot\theta_{k}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{s}\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{8}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}.

Combining the results for
Y_{2a},Y_{2b},Y_{2c}
gives the claim for
\mathrm{Var}(Y_{2})
.

In
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.45)
it is shown that
\mathbb{E}Y_{3}=0
and the decomposition

\displaystyle Y_{3}

\displaystyle=-\frac{2}{\sqrt{v}}\sum_{i,j,k,\ell(dist)}\eta_{i}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}W^{2}_{jk}W_{\ell i}-\frac{2}{\sqrt{v}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,k,\ell(dist)\\
s\notin\{j,k\}\end{subarray}}\eta_{i}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}W_{js}W_{jk}W_{\ell i}

(E.40)

\displaystyle\equiv Y_{3a}+Y_{3b},

(E.41)

is introduced. There it is shown that

\mathrm{Var}(Y_{3a})=\frac{4}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,k,\ell(dist)\\
i^{\prime},j^{\prime},k^{\prime},\ell^{\prime}(dist)\end{subarray}}(\eta_{i}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}\eta_{i^{\prime}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k^{\prime}\ell^{\prime}})\cdot\mathbb{E}[W^{2}_{jk}W_{\ell i}W^{2}_{j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}W_{\ell^{\prime}i^{\prime}}].

Using (
E.1
), (
E.2
) (
E.24
) and the casework in
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.45)
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{3a})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}\bigg(\sum_{ijk\ell}[\beta_{k}^{2}\beta_{\ell}^{2}+\beta_{i}\beta_{j}\beta_{k}\beta_{\ell}]\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{j}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}+\sum_{ijk\ell j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}\beta_{k}\beta_{\ell}^{2}\beta_{k^{\prime}}\theta_{i}^{3}\theta_{j}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{3}\theta_{j^{\prime}}\theta_{k^{\prime}}^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}+\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}

Similar to the study of
Y_{2a}
we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{3b})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{v}\sum_{ijk\ell s}\big(\theta_{i}\beta_{k}\theta_{k}\beta_{\ell}\theta_{\ell}+\theta_{\ell}\beta_{k}\theta_{k}\beta_{i}\theta_{i}+\theta_{i}\beta_{s}\theta_{s}\beta_{\ell}\theta_{\ell}+\theta_{\ell}\beta_{s}\theta_{s}\beta_{i}\theta_{i}\big)^{2}\cdot\mathrm{Var}(W_{sj}W_{jk}W_{\ell i})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{v}\sum_{ijk\ell s}\big(\theta_{i}\beta_{k}\theta_{k}\beta_{\ell}\theta_{\ell}+\theta_{\ell}\beta_{k}\theta_{k}\beta_{i}\theta_{i}+\theta_{i}\beta_{s}\theta_{s}\beta_{\ell}\theta_{\ell}+\theta_{\ell}\beta_{s}\theta_{s}\beta_{i}\theta_{i}\big)^{2}\cdot\theta_{s}\theta_{j}^{2}\theta_{k}\theta_{\ell}\theta_{i}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}.

Combining the bounds on
\mathrm{Var}(Y_{3a})
and
\mathrm{Var}(Y_{3b})
yields the desired bound on
\mathrm{Var}(Y_{3})
.

Following
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.46)
we obtain the decomposition

\displaystyle Y_{4}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{v}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,\ell(dist)\\
s\neq j\end{subarray}}\Bigl(\sum_{k\notin\{i,j,\ell\}}\eta_{i}\widetilde{\Omega}_{jk}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}\Bigr)W_{js}W_{\ell i}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{v}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,\ell(dist)\\
s\neq i\end{subarray}}\Bigl(\sum_{j,k\notin\{i,\ell\}}\eta_{j}\widetilde{\Omega}_{jk}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}\Bigr)W_{is}W_{\ell i}

\displaystyle\equiv Y_{4a}+Y_{4b}.

First we study
Y_{4a}
, which is shown in
Jin et al. 2021c
to have zero mean and satisfy the following:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{4a})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}ij\ell(dist)\\
s\neq j\end{subarray}}\alpha_{ij\ell}^{2}\mathrm{Var}(W_{js}W_{\ell i})

where
\alpha_{ij\ell}=\sum_{k\notin\{i,j,\ell\}}\eta_{i}\tilde{\Omega}_{jk}\tilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}
.
Simlar to previous arguments, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{4a})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}\sum_{ij\ell s}\theta_{i}^{2}(\beta_{j}\theta_{j})^{2}(\beta_{\ell}\theta_{\ell})^{2}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\cdot\theta_{i}\theta_{j}\theta_{\ell}\theta_{s}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}.

Next we study
Y_{4b}
using the decomposition

Y_{4b}=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{v}}\sum_{i,\ell(dist)}\beta_{i\ell}W^{2}_{\ell i}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{v}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,\ell(dist)\\
s\notin\{i,\ell\}\end{subarray}}\beta_{i\ell}W_{is}W_{\ell i}\equiv\widetilde{Y}_{4b}+Y_{4b}^{*}.

from
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement,pg.47)
. There it is shown that only
\mathbb{E}\tilde{Y}_{4b}
is nonzero and

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}\tilde{Y}_{4b}|\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}}\sum_{i,\ell}|\alpha_{i\ell}|\theta_{i}\theta_{\ell}.

where
\alpha_{i,\ell}=\sum_{j,k\notin\{i,\ell\}}\eta_{j}\widetilde{\Omega}_{jk}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}
. In our case, we derive from (
E.24
),

\displaystyle|\alpha_{i\ell}|\lesssim\beta_{\ell}\theta_{\ell}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{3}\|\theta\|_{2}.

Using similar arguments from before,

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}\tilde{Y}_{4b}|\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}}\sum_{i\ell}\beta_{\ell}\theta_{\ell}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{3}\|\theta\|_{2}\cdot\theta_{i}\theta_{\ell}\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}.

Now we study
\mathrm{Var}(Y_{4b})
. Using the bound above on
|\alpha_{i\ell}|
and direct calculations,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\widetilde{Y}_{4b})

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{v}\sum_{i,\ell(dist)}\alpha^{2}_{i\ell}\cdot\mathrm{Var}(W^{2}_{i\ell})\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|^{2}_{1}}\sum_{i,\ell}\beta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{6}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\theta_{i}\theta_{\ell}\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{8}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}}{\|\theta\|_{1}},

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y^{*}_{4b})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,\ell(dist)\\
s\notin\{i,\ell\}\end{subarray}}\alpha^{2}_{i\ell}\cdot\mathrm{Var}(W_{is}W_{\ell i})\leq\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}\sum_{i,\ell,s}\beta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{6}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{\ell}\theta_{s}\leq\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{8}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}.

Combining the results above yields the required bounds on
\mathbb{E}Y_{4b}
and
\mathrm{Var}(Y_{4b})
.

In
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.48)
it is shown that
\mathbb{E}Y_{5}=0
and

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{5})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,k,\ell(dist)\\
s\neq j\end{subarray}}\alpha^{2}_{jk\ell}\cdot\mathrm{Var}(W_{js}W_{k\ell})

where

\displaystyle\alpha_{jk\ell}\equiv\sum_{i\notin\{j,k,\ell\}}\eta_{i}\widetilde{\Omega}_{jk}\widetilde{\Omega}_{\ell i}.

We have using (
E.20
), (
E.24
) and the triangle inequality,

\displaystyle|\alpha_{jk\ell}|\lesssim\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}(\beta_{j}\theta_{j})(\beta_{k}\theta_{k})(\beta_{\ell}\theta_{\ell}).

Thus, by similar arguments to before,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{5})\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}\sum_{jk\ell}\big(\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}(\beta_{j}\theta_{j})^{2}(\beta_{k}\theta_{k})^{2}(\beta_{\ell}\theta_{\ell})^{2}\big)\theta_{j}\theta_{s}\theta_{k}\theta_{\ell}\lesssim\frac{\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}.

Next, in
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.49)
it is shown that
\mathbb{E}Y_{6}=0
and

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{6})

\displaystyle=\frac{8}{v}\sum_{j,s(dist)}\Bigl(\sum_{i,k,\ell(dist)\notin\{j\}}\eta_{i}\widetilde{\Omega}_{jk}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}\widetilde{\Omega}_{\ell i}\Bigr)^{2}\cdot\mathrm{Var}(W_{js}).

We have using (
E.20
), (
E.24
) and the triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\big|\sum_{i,k,\ell(dist)\notin\{j\}}\eta_{i}\widetilde{\Omega}_{jk}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}\widetilde{\Omega}_{\ell i}\big|\lesssim\beta_{j}\theta_{j}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{5}\|\theta\|_{2}.

Thus

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{6})\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}\sum_{j,s}\big(\beta_{j}^{2}\theta_{j}^{2}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{10}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}\big)\theta_{j}\theta_{s}\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{12}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}.

This completes the proof. ∎

E.4.4
Proof of Lemma
E.9

The bounds on
Z_{1}
and
Z_{2}
carry over directly from
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Lemma G.7)
since neither term depends on
\tilde{\Omega}
.

We consider
Z_{3}
. In
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.61)
, the decomposition

\displaystyle Z_{3}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,k,\ell\\
(dist)\end{subarray}}\eta_{i}(\eta_{j}-\tilde{\eta}_{j})\eta_{j}(\eta_{k}-\tilde{\eta}_{k})\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}W_{\ell i}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,k,\ell\\
(dist)\end{subarray}}\eta_{i}(\eta_{j}-\tilde{\eta}_{j})^{2}\eta_{k}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}W_{\ell i}

(E.42)

\displaystyle\hskip-30.0pt+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,k,\ell\\
(dist)\end{subarray}}(\eta_{i}-\tilde{\eta}_{i})\eta^{2}_{j}(\eta_{k}-\tilde{\eta}_{k})\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}W_{\ell i}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,k,\ell\\
(dist)\end{subarray}}(\eta_{i}-\tilde{\eta}_{i})\eta_{j}(\eta_{j}-\tilde{\eta}_{j})\eta_{k}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}W_{\ell i}

(E.43)

\displaystyle\equiv Z_{3a}+Z_{3b}+Z_{3c}+Z_{3d}.

(E.44)

is introduced. We study each term separately.

In
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.61)
it is shown that
\mathbb{E}Z_{3a}=0
and
the decomposition

\displaystyle Z_{3a}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{v}\sum_{i,j,k,\ell(dist)}\alpha_{ijk\ell}W^{2}_{jk}W_{\ell i}+\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,k,\ell(dist)\\
s\neq j,t\neq k,(s,t)\neq(k,j)\end{subarray}}\alpha_{ijk\ell}W_{js}W_{kt}W_{\ell i}

\displaystyle\equiv\widetilde{Z}_{3a}+Z^{*}_{3a}.

is introduced, where
\alpha_{ijk\ell}\equiv\eta_{i}\eta_{j}\tilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}
. Then

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{Z}_{3a})\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}ijk\ell(dist)\\
i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\ell^{\prime}(dist)\end{subarray}}|\alpha_{ijk\ell}||\alpha_{i^{\prime}k^{\prime}j^{\prime}\ell^{\prime}}|\cdot|\mathrm{Cov}(W^{2}_{jk}W_{\ell i},W_{j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}^{2}W_{\ell^{\prime}i^{\prime}})|.

Using the casework in
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.62)
, (
E.1
), (
E.2
), and (
E.24
), we obtain

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{Z}_{3a})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{v^{2}}\big(\sum_{ijk\ell}[\beta_{k}^{2}\beta_{\ell}^{2}+\beta_{k}\beta_{\ell}\beta_{i}\beta_{j}]\theta_{i}^{3}\theta_{j}^{3}\theta_{k}^{3}\theta_{\ell}^{3}+\sum_{ijk\ell j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}\beta_{k}\beta_{\ell}^{2}\beta_{k^{\prime}}\theta_{i}^{3}\theta_{j}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{3}\theta_{j^{\prime}}^{2}\theta_{k^{\prime}}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\big(\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}+\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}+\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{8}\big)\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{8}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{3a}^{*})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{v^{2}}\bigg(\sum_{ijk\ell st}\beta_{k}^{2}\beta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{i}^{3}\theta_{j}^{3}\theta_{k}^{3}\theta_{\ell}^{3}\theta_{s}\theta_{t}+\sum_{ijk\ell st}[\beta_{k}^{2}\beta_{\ell}\beta_{j}+\beta_{k}\beta_{\ell}^{2}\beta_{j}]\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{j}^{3}\theta_{k}^{3}\theta_{\ell}^{3}\theta_{s}^{2}\theta_{t}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\big(\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}+\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}\|\theta\|_{1}\big)\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{3a})\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}.

Next, in
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.63)
, it is shown that
\mathbb{E}Z_{3b}]=0
and the decomposition

Z_{3b}=\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,\ell(dist)\\
s\neq j\end{subarray}}\beta_{ij\ell}W^{2}_{js}W_{\ell i}+\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,\ell(dist)\\
s,t(dist)\notin\{j\}\end{subarray}}\beta_{ij\ell}W_{js}W_{jt}W_{\ell i}\equiv\widetilde{Z}_{3b}+Z^{*}_{3b}.

is given. Using
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.63)
we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{Z}_{3b})

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,\ell,s,t\\
i^{\prime},j^{\prime},\ell^{\prime},s^{\prime},t^{\prime}\end{subarray}}|\alpha_{ij\ell}||\alpha_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}\ell^{\prime}}||\mathrm{Cov}(W_{js}^{2}W_{\ell_{i}},W_{j^{\prime}s}^{\prime 2}W_{\ell^{\prime}i^{\prime}})|.

where

\displaystyle\alpha_{ij\ell}=\sum_{k\notin\{i,j,\ell\}}\eta_{i}\eta_{k}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}.

Using (
E.24
), (
E.18
), and similar arguments to before,

\displaystyle|\alpha_{ij\ell}|\lesssim\theta_{i}(\beta_{\ell}\theta_{\ell})\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}.

By the casework in
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.63)
, (
E.1
), and (
E.2
),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{Z}_{3b})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{v^{2}}\bigg(\sum_{ij\ell s}\beta_{\ell}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\theta_{i}^{3}\theta_{j}\theta_{\ell}^{3}\theta_{s}+\sum_{ij\ell sj^{\prime}s^{\prime}}\beta_{\ell}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\theta_{i}^{3}\theta_{j}\theta_{\ell}^{3}\theta_{s}\theta_{j^{\prime}}\theta_{s^{\prime}}+\sum_{ij\ell s}\beta_{\ell}\beta_{j}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{j}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{s}^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\big(\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}\|\theta\|_{1}+\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}\|\theta\|_{1}^{3}+\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{8}\big)\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}.

By a similar argument,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{3b}^{*})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{8}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}.

Hence by (
E.2
),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{3b})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{8}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}.

For
Z_{3c}
, in
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.64)
, it is shown that
\mathbb{E}Z_{3c}=0
and the decomposition

Z_{3c}=\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,k,\ell(dist)\\
t\neq k\end{subarray}}\alpha_{ik\ell}W^{2}_{i\ell}W_{kt}+\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,k,\ell(dist)\\
s\notin\{i,\ell\},t\neq k\end{subarray}}\alpha_{ik\ell}W_{is}W_{kt}W_{\ell i}\equiv\widetilde{Z}_{3c}+Z^{*}_{3c}.

is given. We have

\displaystyle|\alpha_{ik\ell}|=|\sum_{j\notin\{i,k,\ell\}}\eta_{j}^{2}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}|\lesssim(\beta_{k}\theta_{k})(\beta_{\ell}\theta_{\ell})\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}.

By the casework in
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.65)

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{Z}_{3c})

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}ik\ell(dist)\\
s\notin\{i,\ell\},t\neq k\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime}k^{\prime}\ell^{\prime}(dist)\\
s^{\prime}\notin\{i^{\prime},\ell^{\prime}\},t^{\prime}\neq k^{\prime}\end{subarray}}|\alpha_{ik\ell}\alpha_{i^{\prime}k^{\prime}\ell^{\prime}}||\mathbb{E}W_{i\ell}^{2}W_{kt}W_{i^{\prime}\ell^{\prime}}^{2}W_{k^{\prime}t^{\prime}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\sum_{ik\ell t}\bigg[\beta_{k}^{2}\beta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{i}\theta_{k}^{3}\theta_{\ell}^{3}\theta_{t}+\beta_{k}^{2}\beta_{\ell}\beta_{i}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{k}^{3}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{t}+\beta_{k}\beta_{\ell}^{2}\beta_{t}\theta_{i}^{1}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{3}\theta_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\beta_{k}\beta_{\ell}\beta_{t}\beta_{i}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{t}^{2}+\beta_{k}^{2}\beta_{\ell}\beta_{i}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{k}^{3}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{t}^{1}+\beta_{k}\beta_{\ell}^{2}\beta_{t}\theta_{i}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{3}\theta_{t}^{2}+\beta_{k}^{2}\beta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{i}\theta_{k}^{3}\theta_{\ell}^{3}\theta_{t}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{ik\ell ti^{\prime}\ell^{\prime}}\bigg[\beta_{k}^{2}\beta_{\ell}\beta_{\ell^{\prime}}\theta_{i}\theta_{k}^{3}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{t}\theta_{i^{\prime}}\theta_{\ell^{\prime}}^{2}+\beta_{k}\beta_{\ell}\beta_{\ell^{\prime}}\beta_{t}\theta_{i}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{t}^{2}\theta_{i^{\prime}}\theta_{\ell^{\prime}}^{2}\bigg]

We have by (
E.2
) and (
E.24
) that

\displaystyle\sum_{ik\ell t}\bigg[\beta_{k}^{2}\beta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{i}\theta_{k}^{3}\theta_{\ell}^{3}\theta_{t}+

\displaystyle\beta_{k}^{2}\beta_{\ell}\beta_{i}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{k}^{3}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{t}+\beta_{k}\beta_{\ell}^{2}\beta_{t}\theta_{i}^{1}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{3}\theta_{t}^{2}+\beta_{k}\beta_{\ell}\beta_{t}\beta_{i}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\beta_{k}^{2}\beta_{\ell}\beta_{i}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{k}^{3}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{t}^{1}+\beta_{k}\beta_{\ell}^{2}\beta_{t}\theta_{i}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{3}\theta_{t}^{2}+\beta_{k}^{2}\beta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{i}\theta_{k}^{3}\theta_{\ell}^{3}\theta_{t}\bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}+\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{1}+\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}+\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}

and

\displaystyle\sum_{ik\ell ti^{\prime}\ell^{\prime}}\bigg[\beta_{k}^{2}\beta_{\ell}\beta_{\ell^{\prime}}\theta_{i}\theta_{k}^{3}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{t}\theta_{i^{\prime}}\theta_{\ell^{\prime}}^{2}+\beta_{k}\beta_{\ell}\beta_{\ell^{\prime}}\beta_{t}\theta_{i}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{t}^{2}\theta_{i^{\prime}}\theta_{\ell^{\prime}}^{2}\bigg]\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{1}^{3}+\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}

Thus

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{Z}_{3c})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\big(\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}+\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{1}^{3}+\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}\big)\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{8}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}

To study
Z_{3c}^{*}
, in
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.65)
the decomposition

Z_{3c}^{*}=\frac{1}{v}\sum_{i,k,\ell(dist)}\alpha_{ik\ell}W_{ik}^{2}W_{\ell i}+\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,k,\ell(dist)\\
s\notin\{i,\ell\},t\neq k,(s,t)\neq(k,i)\end{subarray}}\alpha_{ik\ell}W_{is}W_{kt}W_{\ell i}\equiv Z_{3c,1}^{*}+Z_{3c,2}^{*}

is used, where recall
\alpha_{ik\ell}=\sum_{j\notin\{i,k,\ell\}}\eta_{j}^{2}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}
.
Using a similar argument as before, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{3c,1}^{*})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\bigg(\sum_{ik\ell}\beta_{k}^{2}\beta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{k}^{3}\theta_{\ell}^{3}+\sum_{ik\ell k^{\prime}}[\beta_{k}\beta_{k^{\prime}}\beta_{\ell}^{2}+\beta_{k}\beta_{k^{\prime}}\beta_{i}\beta_{\ell}]\theta_{i}^{3}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{3}\theta_{k^{\prime}}^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\big(\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}+\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{3}^{3}+\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\big)\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{10}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}.

We omit the argument for
Z_{3c,2}^{*}
as it is similar and simply state the bound:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{3c,2}^{*})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}.

Combining the results for
\tilde{Z}_{3c}
and
Z_{3c}^{*}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{3c})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{8}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}.

Next we study
Z_{3d}
, which is defined as

Z_{3d}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,k,\ell\\
(dist)\end{subarray}}(\eta_{k}\eta_{j}\tilde{\Omega}_{j\ell})(\eta_{i}-\tilde{\eta}_{i})(\eta_{k}-\tilde{\eta}_{k})W_{\ell i}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,k,\ell(dist)\\
s\neq i,t\neq k\end{subarray}}\alpha_{ik\ell}W_{is}W_{kt}W_{\ell i}

where
\alpha_{ik\ell}=\sum_{j\notin\{i,k,\ell\}}\eta_{k}\eta_{j}\tilde{\Omega}_{j\ell}
. We see that
\mathbb{E}Z_{3d}=0
. To study the variance, we use a similar decomposition to that of
Z_{3c}
. Write

\displaystyle Z_{3d}=\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,k,\ell(dist)\\
t\neq k\end{subarray}}\alpha_{ik\ell}W^{2}_{i\ell}W_{kt}+\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,k,\ell(dist)\\
s\notin\{i,\ell\},t\neq k\end{subarray}}\alpha_{ik\ell}W_{is}W_{kt}W_{\ell i}\equiv\widetilde{Z}_{3d}+Z^{*}_{3d}.

Mimicking the arguments for
\widetilde{Z}_{3c}
and
Z^{*}_{3c}
we obtain

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{Z}_{3d})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}},

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z^{*}_{3d})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{10}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}.

Hence

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{3d})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}.

Combining the results for
Z_{3a},\ldots,Z_{3d}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}Z_{3}=0,\quad\mathrm{Var}(Z_{3})

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}.

We proceed to study
Z_{4}
. In
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement,pg.67)
the following decomposition is given:

\displaystyle Z_{4}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{i,j,k,\ell(dist)}\eta_{i}(\eta_{j}-\tilde{\eta}_{j})\widetilde{\Omega}_{jk}\eta_{k}(\eta_{\ell}-\tilde{\eta}_{\ell})W_{\ell i}

(E.45)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i,j,k,\ell(dist)}\eta_{i}(\eta_{j}-\tilde{\eta}_{j})\widetilde{\Omega}_{jk}(\eta_{k}-\tilde{\eta}_{k})\eta_{\ell}W_{\ell i}

(E.46)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i,j,k,\ell(dist)}(\eta_{i}-\tilde{\eta}_{i})\eta_{j}\widetilde{\Omega}_{jk}\eta_{k}(\eta_{\ell}-\tilde{\eta}_{\ell})W_{\ell i}

(E.47)

\displaystyle\equiv Z_{4a}+Z_{4b}+Z_{4c}.

(E.48)

There it is shown that
\mathbb{E}Z_{4a}=0
. To study
\mathrm{Var}(Z_{4a})
, we note that
Z_{4a}
and
Z_{3c}
have similar structure. In particular we have the decomposition

\displaystyle Z_{4a}=\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,k,\ell(dist)\\
t\neq k\end{subarray}}\alpha_{ik\ell}W^{2}_{i\ell}W_{kt}+\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,k,\ell(dist)\\
s\notin\{i,\ell\},t\neq k\end{subarray}}\alpha_{ik\ell}W_{is}W_{kt}W_{\ell i}\equiv\widetilde{Z}_{4a}+Z^{*}_{4a}.

where
\alpha_{ik\ell}=\sum_{j\notin\{i,k,\ell\}}\eta_{j}\eta_{\ell}\tilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}
.
Mimicking the argument for
\tilde{Z}_{3c}
we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{Z}_{4a})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\bigg(\sum_{ik\ell t}\big[\beta_{k}^{2}(\theta_{i}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{t}+\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{t})+\beta_{k}\beta_{t}\theta_{i}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{t}^{2}+\beta_{k}\beta_{t}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\beta_{k}^{2}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{t}+\beta_{k}\beta_{t}\theta_{i}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{t}^{2}\big]+\sum_{ik\ell ti^{\prime}\ell^{\prime}}\big[\beta_{k}^{2}\theta_{i}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{t}\theta_{i^{\prime}}\theta_{\ell^{\prime}}^{2}+\beta_{k}\beta_{t}\theta_{i}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{t}^{2}\theta_{i^{\prime}}\theta_{\ell^{\prime}}^{2}\big]\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\big(\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{1}+\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{1}+\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{1}^{3}+

\displaystyle\quad\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}\big)\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}.

For
\tilde{Z}_{4a}^{*}
we adapt the decomposition used for
\tilde{Z}_{4c}^{*}
:

Z_{4a}^{*}=\frac{1}{v}\sum_{i,k,\ell(dist)}\alpha_{ik\ell}W_{ik}^{2}W_{\ell i}+\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,k,\ell(dist)\\
s\notin\{i,\ell\},t\neq k,(s,t)\neq(k,i)\end{subarray}}\alpha_{ik\ell}W_{is}W_{kt}W_{\ell i}=:Z_{4a,1}^{*}+Z_{4a,2}^{*}

Mimicking the argument for
Z_{3c,1}^{*}
and
Z_{3c,2}^{*}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{4a,1}^{*})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\big(\sum_{ik\ell}\beta_{k}^{2}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}+\sum_{ik\ell k^{\prime}}\beta_{k}\beta_{k^{\prime}}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{k^{\prime}}^{2}\big)\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{8}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}},

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{4a,2}^{*})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\sum_{ik\ell st}\big[\beta_{k}^{2}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{s}\theta_{t}+\beta_{k}\beta_{t}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{s}\theta_{t}^{2}+\beta_{k}\beta_{s}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{s}^{2}\theta_{t}^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{4a})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}.

Next we study

\displaystyle Z_{4b}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,k,\ell\\
(dist)\end{subarray}}\eta_{i}(\eta_{j}-\tilde{\eta}_{j})\widetilde{\Omega}_{jk}(\eta_{k}-\tilde{\eta}_{k})\eta_{\ell}W_{\ell i}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,k,\ell\\
(dist)\end{subarray}}\alpha_{ijk\ell}(\eta_{j}-\tilde{\eta}_{j})(\eta_{k}-\tilde{\eta}_{k})W_{\ell i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,k,\ell(dist)\\
s\neq j,t\neq k\end{subarray}}\alpha_{ijk\ell}W_{js}W_{kt}W_{\ell i}

where
\alpha_{ijk\ell}=\eta_{i}\eta_{\ell}\widetilde{\Omega}_{jk}
. Mimicking the study of
Z_{3a}
, we have the decomposition

\displaystyle Z_{4b}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{v}\sum_{i,j,k,\ell(dist)}\alpha_{ijk\ell}W^{2}_{jk}W_{\ell i}+\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,k,\ell(dist)\\
s\neq j,t\neq k,(s,t)\neq(k,j)\end{subarray}}\alpha_{ijk\ell}W_{js}W_{kt}W_{\ell i}

\displaystyle\equiv\widetilde{Z}_{4b}+Z^{*}_{4b}.

Further we have, using (
E.1
), (
E.2
), (
E.20
), and (
E.24
), we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{Z}_{4b})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\bigg(\sum_{ijk\ell}\big[\,[\beta_{j}^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}+\beta_{j}\beta_{k}\beta_{\ell}\beta_{i}]\theta_{i}^{3}\theta_{j}^{3}\theta_{k}^{3}\theta_{\ell}^{3}\big]+\sum_{ijk\ell j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}\beta_{j}\beta_{k}\beta_{j^{\prime}}\beta_{k^{\prime}}\theta_{i}^{3}\theta_{j}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{3}\theta_{j^{\prime}}^{2}\theta_{k^{\prime}}^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\big(\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}+\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{8}\big)\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{8}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{4b}^{*})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\bigg(\sum_{ijk\ell st}\big[\beta_{j}^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{j}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{s}\theta_{t}+\beta_{k}^{2}\beta_{\ell}\beta_{j}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{j}^{3}\theta_{k}^{3}\theta_{\ell}^{3}\theta_{s}^{2}\theta_{t}+\beta_{j}\beta_{k}^{2}\beta_{\ell}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{j}^{3}\theta_{k}^{3}\theta_{\ell}^{3}\theta_{s}^{2}\theta_{t}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{4b})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}.

We study
Z_{4c}
using the decomposition

\displaystyle Z_{4c}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{v}\sum_{i,\ell(dist)}\beta_{i\ell}W^{3}_{\ell i}+\frac{2}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,\ell(dist)\\
s\notin\{i,\ell\}\end{subarray}}\beta_{i\ell}W_{is}W^{2}_{\ell i}+\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,\ell(dist)\\
s\notin\{i,\ell\},t\notin\{\ell,i\}\end{subarray}}\beta_{i\ell}W_{is}W_{\ell t}W_{\ell i}

\displaystyle\equiv\widetilde{Z}_{4c}+Z^{*}_{4c}+Z^{{\dagger}}_{4c}.

from
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.68)
. Only

\tilde{Z}_{4c}=\frac{1}{v}\sum_{i,\ell(dist)}\alpha_{i\ell}W^{3}_{\ell i}

has nonzero mean, where
\alpha_{i\ell}=\sum_{j,k(dist)\notin\{i,\ell\}}\eta_{j}\eta_{k}\widetilde{\Omega}_{jk}
. By (
E.20
)

\displaystyle|\alpha_{i\ell}|\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}.

Hence

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}\tilde{Z}_{4c}|\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}\sum_{i\ell}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}\theta_{i}\theta_{\ell}\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}.

Except for when
(i,\ell)=(\ell,i)
, the summands of
\tilde{Z}_{4c}
are uncorrelated. Thus

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{Z}_{4c})\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\sum_{i\ell}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\theta_{i}\theta_{\ell}\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}.

Applying the casework from
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.68)
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{4c}^{*})

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,\ell(dist)\\
s\notin\{i,\ell\}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i^{\prime},\ell^{\prime}(dist)\\
s^{\prime}\notin\{i^{\prime},\ell^{\prime}\}\end{subarray}}|\alpha_{i\ell}\alpha_{i^{\prime}\ell^{\prime}}|\mathrm{Cov}(W_{is}W_{\ell i}^{2},W_{i^{\prime}s^{\prime}}W_{\ell^{\prime}i^{\prime}}^{2})|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\big(\sum_{i\ell s}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{\ell}\theta_{s}+\sum_{i\ell s\ell^{\prime}}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\theta_{i}^{3}\theta_{\ell}\theta_{s}\theta_{\ell^{\prime}}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\big(\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}+\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{1}^{3}\big)\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}.

Next, in
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.69)
it is shown that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z^{{\dagger}}_{4c})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{v^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,\ell(dist)\\
s\notin\{i,\ell\},t\notin\{\ell,i\}\end{subarray}}\alpha^{2}_{i\ell}\cdot\mathrm{Var}(W_{is}W_{\ell t}W_{\ell i})

Thus

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z^{{\dagger}}_{4c})

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i\ell s}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\theta_{i}^{2}\theta_{\ell}^{2}\theta_{s}\theta_{t}\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{8}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}.

Combining the results for
\tilde{Z}_{4c},Z_{4c}^{*},Z_{4c}^{\dagger}
, we have

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}Z_{4c}|\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2},\quad\mathrm{Var}(Z_{4c})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{8}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}.

Combining the results for
Z_{4a},Z_{4b},
and
Z_{4c}
, we have

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}Z_{4}|\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2},\quad\mathrm{Var}(Z_{4})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}}

To study
Z_{5}
, we use the decomposition

\displaystyle Z_{5}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{i,j,k,\ell(dist)}\eta_{i}(\eta_{j}-\tilde{\eta}_{j})\eta_{j}(\eta_{k}-\tilde{\eta}_{k})\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}\widetilde{\Omega}_{\ell i}+\sum_{i,j,k,\ell(dist)}\eta_{i}(\eta_{j}-\tilde{\eta}_{j})^{2}\eta_{k}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}\widetilde{\Omega}_{\ell i}

(E.49)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{i,j,k,\ell(dist)}(\eta_{i}-\tilde{\eta}_{i})\eta^{2}_{j}(\eta_{k}-\tilde{\eta}_{k})\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}\widetilde{\Omega}_{\ell i}

(E.50)

\displaystyle\equiv Z_{5a}+Z_{5b}+Z_{5c}.

(E.51)

from
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg. 70)
. We further decompose
Z_{5a}
as in
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.70)
:

Z_{5a}=\frac{2}{v}\sum_{j,k(dist)}\alpha_{jk}W_{jk}^{2}+\frac{2}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,k(dist)\\
s\neq j,t\neq k,\\
(s,t)\neq(k,j)\end{subarray}}\alpha_{jk}W_{js}W_{kt}\equiv\widetilde{Z}_{5a}+Z^{*}_{5a}.

where
\alpha_{jk}=\sum_{i,\ell(dist)\notin\{j,k\}}\eta_{i}\eta_{j}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}\widetilde{\Omega}_{\ell i}
. Note that by (
E.20
) and (
E.24
),

\displaystyle|\alpha_{jk}|\lesssim\sum_{i\ell}(\beta_{k}\theta_{k})(\beta_{\ell}\theta_{\ell})^{2}(\beta_{i}\theta_{i})\lesssim\theta_{j}(\beta_{k}\theta_{k})\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{3}\|\theta\|_{2}.

Only
\tilde{Z}_{5a}
has nonzero mean. By (
E.1
) and (
E.2
),

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}Z_{5a}|=|\mathbb{E}\tilde{Z}_{5a}|\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}\sum_{jk}\theta_{j}(\beta_{k}\theta_{k})\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{3}\|\theta\|_{2}\cdot\theta_{j}\theta_{k}\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}.

Now we study the variance of
Z_{5a}
. In
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.70)
it is shown that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\widetilde{Z}_{5a})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{v^{2}}\sum_{j,k(dist)}\alpha_{jk}^{2}\,\mathrm{Var}(W^{2}_{jk})

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z^{*}_{5a})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{v^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,k(dist)\\
s\neq j,t\neq k,\\
(s,t)\neq(k,j)\end{subarray}}\alpha^{2}_{jk}\,\mathrm{Var}(W_{js}W_{kt}).

Thus by (
E.2
) and (
E.24
),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\widetilde{Z}_{5a})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{6}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\big(\sum_{jk}\theta_{j}^{3}\beta_{k}^{2}\theta_{k}^{3}\big)\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{8}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z^{*}_{5a})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{6}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\big(\sum_{jk}\theta_{j}^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\cdot\theta_{j}\theta_{s}\theta_{k}\theta_{t}\big)\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{8}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}.

We conclude that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{5a})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{8}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}.

Next we study
Z_{5b}
using the decomposition

Z_{5b}=\frac{1}{v}\sum_{j,s(dist)}\alpha_{j}W^{2}_{js}+\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\\
s,t(dist)\notin\{j\}\end{subarray}}\alpha_{j}W_{js}W_{jt}\equiv\widetilde{Z}_{5b}+Z^{*}_{5b}.

from
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.71)
, where
\alpha_{j}=\sum_{i,k,\ell(dist)\notin\{j\}}\eta_{i}\eta_{k}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}\widetilde{\Omega}_{\ell i}
. Note that by (
E.2
) and (
E.20
),

\displaystyle|\alpha_{j}|\lesssim\sum_{ik\ell}\theta_{i}\theta_{k}(\beta_{k}\theta_{k})(\beta_{\ell}\theta_{\ell})^{2}(\beta_{i}\theta_{i})\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}.

Only
\widetilde{Z}_{5b}
above has nonzero mean, and we have

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}Z_{5b}|=|\mathbb{E}Z_{5b}|\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}\sum_{j,s}\theta_{j}\theta_{s}\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}.

Similarly for the variances,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{Z}_{5b})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{8}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\sum_{js}\theta_{j}\theta_{s}\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{8}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{5b}^{*})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{8}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{4}}\sum_{jst}\theta_{j}^{2}\theta_{s}\theta_{t}\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{8}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}},

and it follows that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{5b})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{8}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}.

Next we study

\displaystyle Z_{5c}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,k,\ell\\
(dist)\end{subarray}}(\eta_{j}-\tilde{\eta}_{j})\eta^{2}_{i}(\eta_{k}-\tilde{\eta}_{k})\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}\widetilde{\Omega}_{\ell j}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,k,\ell\\
(dist)\end{subarray}}(\eta_{i}^{2}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}\widetilde{\Omega}_{\ell j})(\eta_{j}-\tilde{\eta}_{j})(\eta_{k}-\tilde{\eta}_{k})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,k,\ell(dist)\\
s\neq j,t\neq k\end{subarray}}(\eta_{i}^{2}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}\widetilde{\Omega}_{\ell j})W_{js}W_{kt}=\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,k(dist)\\
s\neq j,t\neq k\end{subarray}}\alpha_{jk}W_{js}W_{kt}

where
\alpha_{jk}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,\ell(dist)\\
i,\ell\notin\{j,k\}\end{subarray}}\eta_{i}^{2}\widetilde{\Omega}_{k\ell}\widetilde{\Omega}_{\ell j}
. Note that by (
E.20
) and (
E.18
) ,

\displaystyle|\alpha_{jk}|\lesssim\sum_{i\ell}\theta_{i}^{2}(\beta_{k}\theta_{k})(\beta_{\ell}\theta_{\ell})^{2}(\beta_{j}\theta_{j})\lesssim(\beta_{j}\theta_{j})(\beta_{k}\theta_{k})\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}.

(E.52)

We further decompose

\displaystyle Z_{5c}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,k\\
(dist)\end{subarray}}\alpha_{jk}W_{jk}^{2}+\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,k(dist)\\
s,t\notin\{j,k\}\end{subarray}}\alpha_{jk}W_{js}W_{kt}\equiv\tilde{Z}_{5c}+Z_{5c}^{*}.

Only the first term has nonzero mean. It follows that

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}Z_{5c}|=|\mathbb{E}\tilde{Z}_{5c}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}\sum_{j,k,s,t}(\beta_{j}\theta_{j})(\beta_{k}\theta_{k})\cdot\theta_{j}\theta_{k}\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}.

Note that
Z_{5c}
and
Z_{5a}
have the same form, but with a different setting of the coefficient
\alpha_{jk}
. Mimicking the variance bounds for
Z_{5a}
we obtain the bound

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Z_{5c})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{8}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}.

Combining the previous bounds we obtain

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}Z_{5}|\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2},\quad\mathrm{Var}(Z_{5})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{8}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}.

Next we study
Z_{6}=Z_{6a}+Z_{6b}
as defined in
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.72)
, where

\displaystyle Z_{6a}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,k,\ell\\
(dist)\end{subarray}}(\eta_{i}\eta_{\ell}\widetilde{\Omega}_{j\ell}\widetilde{\Omega}_{ki})(\eta_{j}-\tilde{\eta}_{j})(\eta_{k}-\tilde{\eta}_{k})=\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,k(dist)\\
s\neq j,t\neq k\end{subarray}}\alpha_{jk}^{(6a)}W_{js}W_{kt}

\displaystyle Z_{6b}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j,k,\ell\\
(dist)\end{subarray}}(\eta_{i}\eta_{\ell}\widetilde{\Omega}_{jk}\widetilde{\Omega}_{\ell i})(\eta_{j}-\tilde{\eta}_{j})(\eta_{k}-\tilde{\eta}_{k})=\frac{1}{v}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,k(dist)\\
s\neq j,t\neq k\end{subarray}}\alpha_{jk}^{(6b)}W_{js}W_{kt}

and

\displaystyle\alpha_{jk}^{(6a)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,\ell(dist)\\
i,\ell\notin\{j,k\}\end{subarray}}\eta_{i}\eta_{\ell}\tilde{\Omega}_{jk}\tilde{\Omega}_{\ell i}

\displaystyle\alpha_{jk}^{(6b)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,\ell(dist)\\
i,\ell\notin\{j,k\}\end{subarray}}\eta_{i}\eta_{\ell}\tilde{\Omega}_{j\ell}\tilde{\Omega}_{ki}.

Thus
Z_{6a}
and
Z_{6b}
take the same form as
Z_{5c}
, but with a different setting of
\alpha_{jk}
. Note that by (
E.24
) and similar arguments from before,

\displaystyle\max(|\alpha_{jk}^{(6a)}|,|\alpha_{jk}^{(6b)}|)\lesssim(\beta_{j}\theta_{j})(\beta_{k}\theta_{k})\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2},

which is the same as the upper bound on
|\alpha_{jk}|
associated to
Z_{5c}
given in (
E.52
). It follows that

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}Z_{6}|\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}},\quad\mathrm{Var}(Z_{6})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{8}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}.

We have proved all claims in Lemma
E.9
. ∎

E.4.5
Proof of Lemma
E.10

The terms
T_{1}
and
F
do not depend on
\tilde{\Omega}
, and thus the claimed bounds transfer directly from
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Lemma G.9)
. Thus we focus on
T_{2}
. We use the decomposition
T_{2}=2(T_{2a}+T_{2b}+T_{2c}+T_{2d})
from
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.73)

where

\displaystyle T_{2a}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}(dist)}\eta_{i_{2}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}\big[(\eta_{i_{1}}-\tilde{\eta}_{i_{1}})(\eta_{i_{2}}-\tilde{\eta}_{i_{2}})(\eta_{i_{3}}-\tilde{\eta}_{i_{3}})\big]\cdot\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{4}i_{1}},

\displaystyle T_{2b}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}(dist)}\eta_{i_{2}}\eta_{i_{3}}^{2}\big[(\eta_{i_{1}}-\tilde{\eta}_{i_{1}})(\eta_{i_{2}}-\tilde{\eta}_{i_{2}})(\eta_{i_{4}}-\tilde{\eta}_{i_{4}})\big]\cdot\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{4}i_{1}},

\displaystyle T_{2c}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}(dist)}\eta_{i_{1}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}\big[(\eta_{i_{2}}-\tilde{\eta}_{i_{2}})^{2}(\eta_{i_{3}}-\tilde{\eta}_{i_{3}})\big]\cdot\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{4}i_{1}},

\displaystyle T_{2d}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}(dist)}\eta_{i_{1}}\eta_{i_{3}}^{2}\big[(\eta_{i_{2}}-\tilde{\eta}_{i_{2}})^{2}(\eta_{i_{4}}-\tilde{\eta}_{i_{4}})\big]\cdot\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{4}i_{1}}.

We study each term separately.

For
T_{2a}
, in
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.89)
, we have the decomposition
T_{2a}=X_{a1}+X_{a2}+X_{a3}+X_{b}
where

\displaystyle X_{a1}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{v^{3/2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}(dist)}\sum_{j_{3}\neq i_{3}}\eta_{i_{2}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}W^{2}_{i_{1}i_{2}}W_{i_{3}j_{3}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}i_{4}},

\displaystyle X_{a2}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{v^{3/2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}(dist)}\sum_{j_{2}\neq i_{2}}\eta_{i_{2}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}W^{2}_{i_{1}i_{3}}W_{i_{2}j_{2}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}i_{4}},

\displaystyle X_{a3}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{v^{3/2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}(dist)}\sum_{j_{1}\neq i_{1}}\eta_{i_{2}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}W^{2}_{i_{2}i_{3}}W_{i_{1}j_{1}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}i_{4}},

\displaystyle X_{b}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{v^{3/2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}(dist)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}\\
j_{k}\neq i_{\ell},k,\ell=1,2,3\end{subarray}}\eta_{i_{2}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}W_{i_{1}j_{1}}W_{i_{2}j_{2}}W_{i_{3}j_{3}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}i_{4}}.

There it is shown that
\mathbb{E}T_{2a}=0
. Further it is argued that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(X_{a1})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}X_{a1}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{v^{3}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}(dist)\\
i_{1}^{\prime},i_{2}^{\prime},i_{3}^{\prime},i_{4}^{\prime}(dist)\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{3},j_{3}^{\prime}\\
j_{3}\neq i_{3},j_{3}^{\prime}\neq i_{3}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\eta_{i_{2}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}\eta_{i_{2}^{\prime}}\eta_{i_{3}^{\prime}}\eta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}[W^{2}_{i_{1}i_{2}}W_{i_{3}j_{3}}W^{2}_{i_{1}^{\prime}i_{2}^{\prime}}W_{i_{3}^{\prime}j_{3}^{\prime}}]\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}i_{4}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}^{\prime}i_{4}^{\prime}}

(E.53)

\displaystyle\equiv V_{A}+V_{B}+V_{C},

where the terms
V_{A},V_{B},V_{C}
correspond to the contributions from cases
A,B,C
, respectively, described in
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.89)
. Concretely, the nonzero terms of (
E.53
) fall into three cases:

Case A.

\{i_{1},i_{2}\}=\{i_{3}^{\prime},j_{3}^{\prime}\}
and

\{i_{3},j_{3}\}=\{i_{1}^{\prime},i_{2}^{\prime}\}

Case B.

\{i_{3},j_{3}\}=\{i_{3}^{\prime},j_{3}^{\prime}\}

and
\{i_{1},i_{2}\}=\{i_{1}^{\prime},i_{2}^{\prime}\}

Case C.

\{i_{3},j_{3}\}=\{i_{3}^{\prime},j_{3}^{\prime}\}

and
\{i_{1},i_{2}\}\neq\{i_{1}^{\prime},i_{2}^{\prime}\}
.

Here
V_{A},V_{B},
and
V_{C}
are defined to be the contributions from each case.

Applying (
E.2
), (
E.22
), and (
E.20
),

\displaystyle|\eta_{i_{2}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}\eta_{i_{2}^{\prime}}\eta_{i_{3}^{\prime}}\eta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}i_{4}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}^{\prime}i_{4}^{\prime}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i_{2}}\theta_{i_{3}}\theta_{i_{4}}\theta_{i_{2}^{\prime}}\theta_{i_{3}^{\prime}}\theta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}(\beta_{i_{1}}\theta_{i_{1}})(\beta_{i_{4}}\theta_{i_{4}})(\beta_{i_{1}^{\prime}}\theta_{i_{1}^{\prime}})(\beta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}\theta_{i_{4}^{\prime}})

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i_{2}}\theta_{i_{3}}\theta_{i_{4}}\theta_{i_{2}^{\prime}}\theta_{i_{3}^{\prime}}\theta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}(\beta_{i_{1}}\theta_{i_{1}})(\beta_{i_{4}}\theta_{i_{4}})\theta_{i_{1}^{\prime}}(\beta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}\theta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}).

(E.54)

Note that using the last inequality reduces the required casework while still yielding a good enough bound. Mimicking the casework in Case A of
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.90)
and applying (
E.24
), we have

\displaystyle V_{A}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{6}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},i_{2},i_{3}\\
i_{4},i_{4}^{\prime},j_{3}\end{subarray}}\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}b_{1},b_{2}\\
(b_{1}+b_{2}=1)\end{subarray}}\beta_{i_{1}}\beta_{i_{4}}\beta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}\theta_{i_{1}}^{2+b_{1}}\theta_{i_{2}}^{2+b_{2}}\theta_{i_{3}}^{3}\theta_{j_{3}}^{2}\theta_{i_{4}}^{2}\theta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{6}}\big(\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{3}\|\theta\|_{2}^{3}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{3}^{3}+\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{3}\|\theta\|_{2}^{3}\|\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{3}^{6}\big)\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{3}\|\theta\|_{2}^{9}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{6}}.

Similarly, applying (
E.54
) along with (
E.22
), (
E.20
), and (
E.24
) yields

\displaystyle V_{B}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{6}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},i_{2},i_{3}\\
i_{4},i_{4}^{\prime},j_{3}\end{subarray}}\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{1},c_{2}\\
(c_{1}+c_{2}=1)\end{subarray}}\beta_{i_{1}}\beta_{i_{4}}\beta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}\theta_{i_{1}}^{3}\theta_{i_{2}}^{3}\theta_{i_{3}}^{2+c_{1}}\theta_{j_{3}}^{1+c_{2}}\theta_{i_{2}}^{2}\theta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}^{2}\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{3}\|\theta\|_{2}^{7}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{5}}.

and

\displaystyle V_{C}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}\\
i_{1}^{\prime},i_{2}^{\prime},i_{4}^{\prime},j_{3}\end{subarray}}\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{1},c_{2}\\
(c_{1}+c_{2}=1)\end{subarray}}\beta_{i_{1}}\beta_{i_{4}}\beta_{i_{1}^{\prime}}\beta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}\theta_{i_{1}}^{2}\theta_{i_{2}}^{2}\theta_{i_{3}}^{2+c_{1}}\theta_{j_{3}}^{1+c_{2}}\theta_{i_{4}}^{2}\theta_{i_{1}^{\prime}}^{2}\theta_{i_{2}^{\prime}}^{2}\theta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}^{2}\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{10}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{5}}.

Thus

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(X_{a1})\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}.

The arguments for
X_{a2}
and
X_{a3}
are similar, and the corresponding
V_{A},V_{B},V_{C}
satisfy the same inequalities above. We simply state the bounds:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}X_{a_{2}}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}X_{a3}=0,\quad\mathrm{Var}(X_{a2})\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4},\quad\mathrm{Var}(X_{a3})\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}.

Next we consider
X_{b}
as defined in
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.89)
. We have
\mathbb{E}X_{b}=0
and focus on the variance. In
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.91)
it is shown that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(X_{b})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[X_{b}^{2}]

\displaystyle=v^{-3}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}(dist)\\
i_{1}^{\prime},i_{2}^{\prime},i_{3}^{\prime},i_{4}^{\prime}(dist)\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{3},j_{3}^{\prime}\\
j_{3}\neq i_{3},j_{3}^{\prime}\neq i_{3}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\eta_{i_{2}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}\eta_{i_{2}^{\prime}}\eta_{i_{3}^{\prime}}\eta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}[W_{i_{1}j_{1}}W_{i_{2}j_{2}}W_{i_{3}j_{3}}W_{i_{1}^{\prime}j_{1}^{\prime}}W_{i_{2}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime}}W_{i_{3}^{\prime}j_{3}^{\prime}}]\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}i_{4}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}^{\prime}i_{4}^{\prime}},

Note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[W_{i_{1}j_{1}}W_{i_{2}j_{2}}W_{i_{3}j_{3}}W_{i_{1}^{\prime}j_{1}^{\prime}}W_{i_{2}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime}}W_{i_{3}^{\prime}j_{3}^{\prime}}]\neq 0

if and only if the two sets of random variables
\{W_{i_{1}j_{1}},W_{i_{2}j_{2}},W_{i_{3}j_{3}}\}
and
\{W_{i_{1}^{\prime}j_{1}^{\prime}},W_{i_{2}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime}},W_{i_{3}^{\prime}j_{3}^{\prime}}\}
are identical. Applying (
E.22
) and (
E.20
),

\displaystyle|\eta_{i_{2}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}\eta_{i_{2}^{\prime}}\eta_{i_{3}^{\prime}}\eta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}i_{4}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}^{\prime}i_{4}^{\prime}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i_{2}}\theta_{i_{3}}\theta_{i_{4}}\theta_{i_{2}^{\prime}}\theta_{i_{3}^{\prime}}\theta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}(\beta_{i_{1}}\theta_{i_{1}})(\beta_{i_{4}}\theta_{i_{4}})\theta_{i_{1}^{\prime}}(\beta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}\theta_{i_{4}^{\prime}})

\displaystyle\lesssim\beta_{i_{1}}\beta_{i_{4}}\beta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}\theta_{i_{1}}^{1+a_{1}}\theta_{j_{1}}^{a_{2}}\theta_{i_{2}}^{1+a_{3}}\theta_{j_{2}}^{a_{4}}\theta_{i_{3}}^{1+a_{5}}\theta_{j_{3}}^{a_{6}}\theta_{i_{4}}^{2}\theta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}^{2}

if
\mathbb{E}[W_{i_{1}j_{1}}W_{i_{2}j_{2}}W_{i_{3}j_{3}}W_{i_{1}^{\prime}j_{1}^{\prime}}W_{i_{2}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime}}W_{i_{3}^{\prime}j_{3}^{\prime}}]\neq 0
, where
a_{i}\in\{0,1\}
and
\sum_{i=1}^{6}a_{i}=3
. Thus by (
E.1
), (
E.2
), and (
E.24
),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(X_{b})

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{a}\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{6}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}\\
i_{4}^{\prime},j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}\end{subarray}}\beta_{i_{1}}\beta_{i_{4}}\beta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}\theta_{i_{1}}^{2+a_{1}}\theta_{j_{1}}^{1+a_{2}}\theta_{i_{2}}^{2+a_{3}}\theta_{j_{2}}^{1+a_{4}}\theta_{i_{3}}^{2+a_{5}}\theta_{j_{3}}^{1+a_{6}}\theta_{i_{4}}^{2}\theta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{6}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}\\
i_{4}^{\prime},j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}\end{subarray}}\beta_{i_{1}}\beta_{i_{4}}\beta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}\theta_{i_{1}}^{2}\theta_{j_{1}}^{1}\theta_{i_{2}}^{2}\theta_{j_{2}}^{1}\theta_{i_{3}}^{2}\theta_{j_{3}}^{1}\theta_{i_{4}}^{2}\theta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{3}\|\theta\|_{2}^{3}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{1}^{3}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{6}}\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{3}\|\theta\|_{2}^{7}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{3}}\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{3}\|\theta\|_{2}.

Combining the results for
X_{a1},X_{a2},X_{a3}
and
X_{b}
, we conclude that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}T_{2a}=0,\qquad\mathrm{Var}(T_{2a})\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}.

The argument for
T_{2b}
is similar to the one for
T_{2a}
, so we simply state the results:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}T_{2b}=0,\qquad\mathrm{Var}(T_{2b})\lesssim\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}.

Next we study
T_{2c}
, providing full details for completeness. Using the definition of
T_{2c}
in
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement, pg.92)
, we have the following decomposition by careful casework.

\displaystyle Y_{a}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{v^{3/2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}(dist)}\eta_{i_{1}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}W^{3}_{i_{2}i_{3}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}i_{4}},

\displaystyle Y_{b1}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{v^{3/2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}(dist)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(i_{2},j_{2})\neq(j_{3},i_{3})\\
j_{2}\neq i_{2},j_{3}\neq i_{3}\end{subarray}}\eta_{i_{1}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}W^{2}_{i_{2}j_{2}}W_{i_{3}j_{3}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}i_{4}},

\displaystyle Y_{b2}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{v^{3/2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}(dist)}\sum_{\ell_{2}\notin\{i_{3},i_{2}\}}\eta_{i_{1}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}W^{2}_{i_{2}i_{3}}W_{i_{2}\ell_{2}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}i_{4}},

\displaystyle Y_{b3}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{v^{3/2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}(dist)}\sum_{j_{2}\notin\{i_{3},i_{2}\}}\eta_{i_{1}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}W^{2}_{i_{2}i_{3}}W_{i_{2}j_{2}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}i_{4}},

\displaystyle Y_{c}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{v^{3/2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}(dist)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{2},\ell_{2},j_{3}\\
j_{2}\neq i_{2},\ell_{2}\neq i_{2},j_{3}\neq i_{3}\\
j_{2}\neq\ell_{2},(i_{2},j_{2})\neq(j_{3},i_{3}),(i_{2},\ell_{2})\neq(j_{3},i_{3})\end{subarray}}\eta_{i_{1}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}W_{i_{2}j_{2}}W_{i_{2}\ell_{2}}W_{i_{3}j_{3}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}i_{4}}.

Note that, by the change of variables
\ell_{2}\to j_{2}
, it holds that
Y_{b2}=Y_{b3}
.

The only term with nonzero mean is
Y_{a}
. We have by (
E.18
), (
E.20
), (
E.22
), and (
E.24
) that

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}Y_{a}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{3}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}}\theta_{i_{1}}\theta_{i_{3}}\theta_{i_{4}}(\beta_{i_{1}}\theta_{i_{1}})(\beta_{i_{4}}\theta_{i_{4}})\cdot|\mathbb{E}W_{i_{2}i_{3}}^{3}|\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{3}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}}\beta_{i_{1}}\beta_{i_{4}}\theta_{i_{1}}^{2}\theta_{i_{2}}\theta_{i_{3}}^{2}\theta_{i_{4}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}.

For the variance, by independence of
\{W_{ij}\}_{i>j}
, (
E.2
), (
E.20
), and (
E.24
), we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{a})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{6}}\sum_{i_{2},i_{3}}\big(\sum_{i_{1},i_{4}}\theta_{i_{1}}\theta_{i_{3}}\theta_{i_{4}}(\beta_{i_{1}}\theta_{i_{1}})(\beta_{i_{4}}\theta_{i_{4}})\big)^{2}\theta_{i_{2}}\theta_{i_{3}}\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{6}}\sum_{i_{2},i_{3}}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\theta_{i_{2}}\theta_{i_{3}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{5}}.

For
Y_{b1},Y_{b2},Y_{b3}
we make note of the identity

\displaystyle W_{ij}^{2}

\displaystyle=(1-2\Omega_{ij})W_{ij}+\Omega_{ij}(1-\Omega_{ij})\equiv A_{ij}W_{ij}+B_{ij}.

(E.55)

Write

\displaystyle Y_{b1}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{v^{3/2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}(dist)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(i_{2},j_{2})\neq(j_{3},i_{3})\\
j_{2}\neq i_{2},j_{3}\neq i_{3}\end{subarray}}\eta_{i_{1}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}A_{i_{2}j_{2}}W_{i_{2}j_{2}}W_{i_{3}j_{3}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}i_{4}}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{v^{3/2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}(dist)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(i_{2},j_{2})\neq(j_{3},i_{3})\\
j_{2}\neq i_{2},j_{3}\neq i_{3}\end{subarray}}\eta_{i_{1}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}B_{i_{2}j_{2}}W_{i_{3}j_{3}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}i_{4}}\equiv Y_{b1,A}+Y_{b1,B}.

By similar arguments from before, and noting that
|A_{i_{2},j_{2}}|\lesssim 1
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{b1,A})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{6}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(i_{2},j_{2})\neq(j_{3},i_{3})\\
j_{2}\neq i_{2},j_{3}\neq i_{3}\end{subarray}}\bigg(\sum_{i_{1},i_{4}}\eta_{i_{1}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}(\beta_{i_{1}}\theta_{i_{1}})(\beta_{i_{4}}\theta_{i_{4}})\bigg)^{2}|\mathbb{E}W_{i_{2}j_{2}}W_{i_{3}j_{3}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{6}}\sum_{i_{2},j_{2},i_{3},j_{3}}\bigg(\sum_{i_{1},i_{4}}\eta_{i_{1}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}(\beta_{i_{1}}\theta_{i_{1}})(\beta_{i_{4}}\theta_{i_{4}})\bigg)^{2}\cdot\theta_{i_{2}}\theta_{j_{2}}\theta_{i_{3}}\theta_{j_{3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{6}}\sum_{i_{2},j_{2},i_{3},j_{3}}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\theta_{i_{2}}\theta_{j_{2}}\theta_{i_{3}}^{3}\theta_{j_{3}}\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{3}}.

Similarly, using
|B_{ij}|\lesssim\Omega_{ij}\lesssim\theta_{i}\theta_{j}
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{b1,B})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{6}}\sum_{i_{3},j_{3}(dist)}\bigg(\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{4},j_{2}}\eta_{i_{1}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}\theta_{i_{2}}\theta_{j_{2}}(\beta_{i_{1}}\theta_{i_{1}})(\beta_{i_{4}}\theta_{i_{4}})\bigg)^{2}\cdot|\mathbb{E}W_{i_{3},j_{3}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{6}}\sum_{i_{3},j_{3}}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}\theta_{i_{3}}^{3}\theta_{j_{3}}\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{3}}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{b1})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{3}}

To control
\mathrm{Var}(Y_{b2})
, again we invoke the identity (
E.55
) to write

\displaystyle Y_{b2}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{v^{3/2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}(dist)}\sum_{\ell_{2}\notin\{i_{3},i_{2}\}}\eta_{i_{1}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}A_{i_{2}i_{3}}W_{i_{2}i_{3}}W_{i_{2}\ell_{2}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}i_{4}}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{v^{3/2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}(dist)}\sum_{\ell_{2}\notin\{i_{3},i_{2}\}}\eta_{i_{1}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}B_{i_{2}i_{3}}W_{i_{2}\ell_{2}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}i_{4}}\equiv Y_{b2,A}+Y_{b2,B}.

Using similar arguments from before, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{b2,A})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{6}}\sum_{i_{2}i_{3}\ell_{2}}\bigg(\sum_{i_{1}i_{4}}\theta_{i_{1}}\theta_{i_{3}}\theta_{i_{4}}(\beta_{i_{1}}\theta_{i_{1}})(\beta_{i_{4}}\theta_{i_{4}})\bigg)^{2}\theta_{i_{2}}^{2}\theta_{i_{3}}\theta_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{6}}\sum_{i_{2}i_{3}\ell_{2}}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}\theta_{i_{2}}^{2}\theta_{i_{3}}^{3}\theta_{\ell_{2}}\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{8}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{5}}.

Furthermore,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{b2,B})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{6}}\sum_{i_{2},\ell_{2}}\bigg(\sum_{i_{1},i_{3},i_{4}}\theta_{i_{1}}\theta_{i_{3}}\theta_{i_{4}}(\beta_{i_{1}}\theta_{i_{1}})(\beta_{i_{4}}\theta_{i_{4}})\theta_{i_{2}}\theta_{i_{3}}\bigg)^{2}\theta_{i_{2}}\theta_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{6}}\sum_{i_{2},\ell_{2}}\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{8}\theta_{i_{2}}^{3}\theta_{\ell_{2}}\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{10}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{5}}.

Since
Y_{b2}=Y_{b3}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{b2})=\mathrm{Var}(Y_{b3})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{10}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{5}}.

Next we study the variance of
Y_{2c}
. For notational brevity, let

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3}}=\bigg\{(j_{2},\ell_{2},j_{3})\bigg|j_{2}\neq i_{2},\ell_{2}\neq i_{2},j_{3}\neq i_{3}j_{2}\neq\ell_{2},(i_{2},j_{2})\neq(j_{3},i_{3}),(i_{2},\ell_{2})\neq(j_{3},i_{3})\bigg\}.

We have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{c})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{v^{3}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}(dist)\\
i_{1}^{\prime},i_{2}^{\prime},i_{3}^{\prime},i_{4}^{\prime}(dist)\end{subarray}}\,\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(j_{2},\ell_{2},j_{3})\in\mathcal{R}_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3}}\\
(j_{2}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime},j_{3}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{R}_{i_{1}^{\prime},i_{2}^{\prime},i_{3}^{\prime}}\end{subarray}}\eta_{i_{1}}\eta_{i_{3}}\eta_{i_{4}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}i_{4}}\eta_{i_{1}^{\prime}}\eta_{i_{3}^{\prime}}\eta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}\widetilde{\Omega}_{i_{1}^{\prime}i_{4}^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}\big[W_{i_{2}j_{2}}W_{i_{2}\ell_{2}}W_{i_{3}j_{3}}W_{i_{2}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime}}W_{i_{2}^{\prime}\ell_{2}^{\prime}}W_{i_{3}^{\prime}j_{3}^{\prime}}\big]

(E.56)

Note that
W_{i_{2}j_{2}}W_{i_{2}\ell_{2}}W_{i_{3}j_{3}}
and
W_{i_{2}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime}}W_{i_{2}^{\prime}\ell_{2}^{\prime}}W_{i_{3}^{\prime}j_{3}^{\prime}}
above are uncorrelated unless

\bigg\{\{i_{2},j_{2}\},\{i_{2},\ell_{2}\},\{i_{3},j_{3}\}\bigg\}=\bigg\{\{i_{2}^{\prime},j_{2}^{\prime}\},\{i_{2}^{\prime},\ell_{2}^{\prime}\},\{i_{3}^{\prime},j_{3}^{\prime}\}\bigg\}.

In particular,
i_{3}^{\prime}\in\{i_{2},j_{2},\ell_{2},i_{3},j_{3}\}
when the above holds. Hence for some choice of
a_{i}\in\{0,1\}
with
\sum_{i=1}^{5}a_{i}=1
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(Y_{c})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{v^{3}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}\\
i_{1}^{\prime},i_{4}^{\prime},j_{2},\ell_{2},j_{3}\end{subarray}}\theta_{i_{2}}^{a_{1}}\theta_{j_{2}}^{a_{2}}\theta_{\ell_{2}}^{a_{3}}\theta_{i_{3}}^{a_{4}}\theta_{j_{3}}^{a_{5}}\cdot\theta_{i_{1}}\theta_{i_{3}}\theta_{i_{4}}(\beta_{i_{1}}\theta_{i_{1}})(\beta_{i_{4}}\theta_{i_{4}})\theta_{i_{1}^{\prime}}\theta_{i^{\prime}_{4}}(\beta_{i_{1}^{\prime}}\theta_{i_{1}^{\prime}})(\beta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}\theta_{i_{4}^{\prime}})\cdot\theta_{i_{2}}^{2}\theta_{j_{2}}\theta_{\ell_{2}}\theta_{i_{3}}\theta_{j_{3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{v^{3}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}\\
i_{1}^{\prime},i_{4}^{\prime},j_{2},\ell_{2},j_{3}\end{subarray}}\beta_{i_{1}}\beta_{i_{1}^{\prime}}\beta_{i_{4}}\beta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}\theta_{i_{1}}^{2}\theta_{i_{2}}^{2+a_{1}}\theta_{i_{3}}^{2+a_{4}}\theta_{i_{4}}^{2}\theta_{i_{1}^{\prime}}^{2}\theta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}^{2}\theta_{j_{2}}^{1+a_{2}}\theta_{\ell_{2}}^{1+a_{3}}\theta_{j_{3}}^{1+a_{5}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{v^{3}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}\\
i_{1}^{\prime},i_{4}^{\prime},j_{2},\ell_{2},j_{3}\end{subarray}}\beta_{i_{1}}\beta_{i_{1}^{\prime}}\beta_{i_{4}}\beta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}\theta_{i_{1}}^{2}\theta_{i_{2}}^{2}\theta_{i_{3}}^{2}\theta_{i_{4}}^{2}\theta_{i_{1}^{\prime}}^{2}\theta_{i_{4}^{\prime}}^{2}\theta_{j_{2}}^{1}\theta_{\ell_{2}}^{1}\theta_{j_{3}}^{1}\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{8}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{3}},

where in the last line we apply (
E.2
) followed by (
E.24
). Combining our results above we have

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}T_{2c}|\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}},\qquad\mathrm{Var}(T_{2c})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{6}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}}.

The argument for
T_{2d}
is omitted since it is similar to the one for
T_{2c}
(note that the two terms have similar structure). The results are stated below.

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}T_{2d}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}},\qquad\mathrm{Var}(T_{2d})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{8}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{3}}.

Combining the results for
T_{2a},\ldots,T_{2d}
yields

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}T_{2}|\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{2}\|\theta\|_{2}^{4}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}},\qquad\mathrm{Var}(T_{2})\lesssim\frac{\|\beta\circ\theta\|_{2}^{4}\|\theta\|_{2}^{8}}{\|\theta\|_{1}^{2}},

as desired. ∎

E.4.6
Proof of Lemma
E.11

As before, we only need to analyze the alternative hypothesis. In
(
Jin et al. 2021c
, Supplement,pg.103)
it is shown that
